# Full Native Workload Catalog Benchmark: Doe C vs Dawn (2026-11-01)

Scope: full 127-workload native catalog (`bench/workloads/metadata/backend-workload-catalog.json`)
evaluated across all four available hardware backends (`apple-metal`, `intel-vulkan`,
`windows-d3d12`, `windows-amd-vulkan`; `excluded-host` omitted per operator instruction).

## 1. Executive Summary & Frozen Contracts

| Backend | Host Class | Contract SHA-256 | Supported / 127 | Unsupported | Operation Losses (`warm.operation_ms` > 1.05x) | All Phase Losses (> 1.05x) | Median `dawn-aa/dawn` (`warm.op`) |
| --- | --- | --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| `apple-metal` | macOS Metal (apple-host, Apple M3) | `20eae73f44ae1724...` | 120 | 7 | 41 | 442 | 1.000x |
| `intel-vulkan` | Linux Intel Vulkan (intel-host, Intel Arc / ANV) | `abdf401974f782fc...` | 119 | 8 | 37 | 390 | 0.999x |
| `windows-d3d12` | Windows D3D12 (amd-windows-host, AMD Radeon) | `3312353a1b070426...` | 88 | 39 | 28 | 383 | 1.000x |
| `windows-amd-vulkan` | Windows AMD Vulkan (amd-windows-host, AMD Radeon) | `49838cb9ac7571b3...` | 91 | 36 | 23 | 194 | 1.000x |

### Methodology
- **Source parity:** Doe C (`libwebgpu_doe_c` + `libdoe_compiler`) and Dawn (`d04f88359391a12375c1a3e948a0b2dcb4c97a18`) compiled in Release mode and linked against identical `runtime/c/runtime-cli` driver harnesses (`doe-c-runtime`, `dawn-c-runtime`, and byte-identical `dawn-aa-c-runtime` A/A control).
- **Counterbalanced pairing:** Each pair executes all 3 arms (`doe`, `dawn`, `dawn-aa`) cycling through all 6 permutations (`(doe,dawn,dawn-aa)`, `(dawn,dawn-aa,doe)`, `(dawn-aa,doe,dawn)`, `(doe,dawn-aa,dawn)`, `(dawn,doe,dawn-aa)`, `(dawn-aa,dawn,doe)`). Adaptive sampling runs `7..11` pairs (2,000 bootstrap resamples, `seed=20261101`) targeting 5% relative CI half-width.
- **Cold and warm attribution:** Command-stream workloads run with `--command-repeat 2` within each process so pass 0 captures cold setup/encode/submit-wait/operation/complete and pass 1 captures warm steady-state execution. Plan workloads (`inference_gemma3_*`, `compute_stable_fluids_*`) separate first-touch kernel compilation steps (cold) from repeated dispatch steps (warm) in `trace.jsonl`.
- **Host lock & quiet-window discipline:**
  - `apple-metal` (`apple-host`): held `flock /tmp/doe-gpu-timing.lock` per window.
  - `intel-vulkan` (`intel-host`): held `flock /tmp/doe-gpu-timing.lock` per window with desktop ChatGPT paused (`SIGSTOP`) during timed windows and resumed (`SIGCONT`) immediately after.
  - `windows-d3d12` & `windows-amd-vulkan` (`amd-windows-host`): waited for `FawnBenchRun` completion, held atomic directory lock `doe-gpu-lock` per window, and verified zero active compiler/linker processes (`clang-cl.exe`, `cl.exe`, `ninja.exe`, `siso.exe`, `lld-link.exe`, `link.exe`).

| `apple-metal` | `texture_sampling_raster_baseline` | `commands` | `texture-raster` | `exit_1` | `skipped_after_doe_fail` | Command 5 (`render_draw`) references `targetHandle: 7103` without a preceding `texture_write` creating handle `7103` (`render_target_missing`). |
| `apple-metal` | `upload_write_buffer_4gb` | `commands` | `upload` | `ok` | `exit_-5` | Allocates a 4 GiB (`4,294,967,296` B) buffer, exceeding adapter/Dawn `maxBufferSize` (`dawnStatus=exit_-5`). |
| `apple-metal` | `upload_write_buffer_4gb_staged` | `commands` | `upload` | `ok` | `exit_-5` | Allocates a 4 GiB (`4,294,967,296` B) buffer, exceeding adapter/Dawn `maxBufferSize` (`dawnStatus=exit_-5`). |

## 3. Losses Grouped by Shared Owner (`file:line` Attribution)

### 3.1 `owner_render_draw_bundle_encoding` — Render Pass / Render Bundle Per-Draw Packet Allocation, Validation, Refcounting, State Re-binding & Draw-Guard Scratch Churn

- **Affected Backends:** `apple-metal`, `intel-vulkan`, `windows-amd-vulkan`, `windows-d3d12`
- **Loss Count:** 110 operation losses (up to `105.9x`), 622 total phase/resource losses (up to `131.8x`) across 24 distinct workloads
- **Doe Code Owners (`file:line`):**
  - `runtime/c/resources/src/render.c:143-171` (`resource_render_packet_retain`, `resource_render_packet_release`) and `runtime/c/resources/src/render.c:474-501` (`append_packet`): validates targets + draw state, heap-allocates a `doe_resource_render_record` node, and executes 24 atomic `atomic_fetch_add` / `atomic_fetch_sub` reference count operations on every single draw call.
  - `runtime/c/resources/src/bundles.c:279-293` (`doe_resource_render_pass_execute_bundles`): validates all bundle draws during `ExecuteBundles` preflight (`bundles.c:279-282`), then replays each packet through `resource_render_replay_packet` -> `append_packet` (`bundles.c:288-293`, re-validating and heap-allocating a new `doe_resource_render_record` per draw), followed by a third validation pass at `wgpuCommandEncoderFinish` (`render.c:661-666`).
  - `runtime/c/native/src/metal_graphics_commands.c:137-147` (`native_metal_render`), `runtime/c/backends/metal/src/render_commands.c:31-90` (`doe_mtl_render_draw_retain`, `doe_mtl_commands_render`), and `runtime/c/backends/metal/src/objc_render_draws.c:4-63` (`stage_bindings`, `doe_mtl_objc_render_encode_draw`): allocates `calloc(draws_count, sizeof(draw_storage))` + `calloc(draws_count, sizeof(doe_mtl_native_render_draw))` (~1.5 GiB per pass for 200k draws), walks linked lists of used pipelines/buffers per draw, and executes 362 Objective-C `objc_msgSend` calls per draw (clearing all 31 vertex buffer, 31 fragment buffer, 128 vertex texture, 128 fragment texture, 16 vertex sampler, and 16 fragment sampler slots, and unconditionally re-sending `setRenderPipelineState:`, `setDepthStencilState:`, `setViewport:`, `setScissorRect:`, `setFrontFacingWinding:`, `setCullMode:`, `setDepthClipMode:`, `setBlendColorRed:...`, `setDepthBias:...`, `setStencilReferenceValue:`).
  - `runtime/c/backends/metal/src/draw_guard.c:144-195` (`doe_mtl_draw_guard_prepare`), `runtime/c/backends/vulkan/src/draw_indirect.c:69-94` (`doe_vk_draw_indirect_prepare`), and `runtime/c/native/src/d3d12_draw_prepare.c:59-132` (`native_d3d12_draw_prepare`): allocates a fresh GPU scratch buffer (`doe_mtl_buffer_create` / `doe_vk_buffer_create` / two `doe_dx_buffer_create` calls), copies 16-20 bytes, and dispatches a compute validation shader per indirect draw (and on D3D12, per indexed draw without consecutive deduplication in `runtime/c/native/src/d3d12_graphics_commands.c:146`, exhausting VRAM on `render_draw_indexed_200k`).
  - `runtime/c/backends/vulkan/src/descriptors.c:202-222` (`same_buffer_use`, `doe_vk_prepare_resources`) and `runtime/c/backends/vulkan/src/render_commands.c:180-236` (`prepare_draw`, `record_draw`): rejects descriptor-set reuse whenever `pipeline->graphics` is true (`descriptors.c:204`), allocating a fresh `doe_vk_dispatch_use`, descriptor set, `vkUpdateDescriptorSets`, and `doe_vk_barrier` per draw, and unconditionally re-binding pipeline, descriptor sets, blend constants, viewport, scissor, and vertex/index buffers on every draw.
  - `runtime/c/backends/d3d12/src/windows_render_commands.c:120-210`: unconditionally re-binds PSO, root signature, descriptor tables, viewport, scissor, blend factor, and stencil reference on every draw.
- **Dawn Code Counterparts (`file:line`):**
  - `bench/vendor/dawn/src/dawn/native/RenderEncoderBase.cpp`, `src/dawn/native/CommandAllocator.cpp`, `src/dawn/native/CommandBufferStateTracker.cpp`: bump-allocates compact command tokens into chunked blocks without per-draw heap allocations or atomic refcount loops when bindings are unchanged.
  - `bench/vendor/dawn/src/dawn/native/metal/CommandBufferMTL.mm`, `src/dawn/native/vulkan/CommandBufferVk.cpp`, `src/dawn/native/d3d12/CommandBufferD3D12.cpp`: tracks dirty pipeline, descriptor/bindgroup, vertex/index buffer, and dynamic state bits across draws and batches indirect validation.
- **Top Affected Workload Losses in This Group:**

### 3.2 `owner_adapter_capability_introspection` — Uncached Adapter Feature & Format Capability Queries on Every `wgpuAdapterGetFeatures` / `wgpuAdapterHasFeature` Call

- **Affected Backends:** `apple-metal`, `intel-vulkan`, `windows-amd-vulkan`, `windows-d3d12`
- **Loss Count:** 12 operation losses (up to `561.0x`), 45 total phase/resource losses (up to `562.2x`) across 2 distinct workloads
- **Doe Code Owners (`file:line`):**
  - `runtime/c/native/src/features.c:135-224` (`native_adapter_features`), `runtime/c/native/src/features.c:268-285` (`wgpuAdapterGetFeatures`, `wgpuAdapterHasFeature`), and `runtime/c/native/src/feature_formats.c:9-58` (`vulkan_tier`, `native_format_adapter_features`): recomputes `native_adapter_features(&adapter->backend)` from scratch on every `wgpuAdapterGetFeatures` and `wgpuAdapterHasFeature` call, invoking ~250 `doe_vk_texture_format_caps` (`vkGetPhysicalDeviceFormatProperties`) calls per query (~2,000,000 driver format queries in `capability_introspection_500`).
- **Dawn Code Counterparts (`file:line`):**
  - `bench/vendor/dawn/src/dawn/native/PhysicalDevice.cpp` and `src/dawn/native/Adapter.cpp` (`APIGetFeatures`, `APIHasFeature`): computes `mSupportedFeatures` once during physical device initialization and serves `GetFeatures` / `HasFeature` from the cached bitset in O(1).
- **Top Affected Workload Losses in This Group:**

### 3.3 `owner_shader_double_compile` — Double WGSL Validation in `doe_shader_create_module` and Double Target Compilation in `shader_compile`

- **Affected Backends:** `apple-metal`, `intel-vulkan`, `windows-amd-vulkan`, `windows-d3d12`
- **Loss Count:** 7 operation losses (up to `9.69x`), 67 total phase/resource losses (up to `258.3x`) across 17 distinct workloads
- **Doe Code Owners (`file:line`):**
  - `runtime/c/shader/src/module.c:41-44` and `runtime/c/shader/src/module.c:93-96` (`doe_shader_create_module`): calls `doe_compiler_validate_module_diagnostics` twice — first with `NULL` output buffers to count diagnostics, entries, and resources, and a second time after allocation, running the WGSL lexer, parser, and semantic typechecker twice per `wgpuDeviceCreateShaderModule`.
  - `runtime/c/shader/src/shader.c:135-148` and `runtime/c/shader/src/shader.c:291-303` (`shader_compile`): calls `specialize` / `compile_text` / `doe_compiler_compile_stages` twice — first with `NULL` output buffers to measure `required_text_bytes` or `required_words`, and a second time after allocation to emit MSL/HLSL/SPIR-V.
  - `runtime/c/compiler/src/cli_text.c:69-94` (`emit_msl_text`, `emit_hlsl_text`) and `runtime/c/compiler/src/cli.c` (`emit_spirv_words`): standalone compiler CLI also invokes the compiler twice (sizing pass + emission pass).

### 3.5 `owner_upload_eager_host_zeroing` — Eager Host-Visible Buffer Allocation & Synchronous Zero-Fill in `wgpuDeviceCreateBuffer` (`setup_ms`) vs Deferred Staging (`submit_wait_ms`)

- **Affected Backends:** `apple-metal`, `intel-vulkan`, `windows-amd-vulkan`, `windows-d3d12`
- **Loss Count:** 26 operation losses (up to `79.30x`), 159 total phase/resource losses (up to `5529.5x`) across 14 distinct workloads
- **Doe Code Owners (`file:line`):**
  - `runtime/c/backends/metal/src/resources.c:26-36` (`doe_mtl_buffer_create`), `runtime/c/backends/vulkan/src/resources.c:40-92` (`doe_vk_buffer_create`), `runtime/c/backends/d3d12/src/windows_resources.c`, and `runtime/c/runtime-cli/src/execute.c:558-591` (`execute_upload`): allocates shared/host-visible memory and synchronously faults/zeroes it (`memset`) during `wgpuDeviceCreateBuffer` (timed in `setup_ms`), making `wgpuQueueWriteBuffer` (`submit_wait_ms`) a fast `memmove` (`0.42x` of Dawn on 1 GiB Metal upload), whereas Dawn creates an unmapped device buffer in `setup_ms` and allocates staging memory in `wgpuQueueWriteBuffer` (`submit_wait_ms`). On small D3D12 uploads (`upload_write_buffer_1kb` / `1mb`), `runtime/c/native/src/d3d12_writes.c:10-46` allocates a temporary `DOE_DX_UPLOAD` committed resource + `doe_dx_commands_copy` + `doe_dx_commands_submit` + fence wait per `wgpuQueueWriteBuffer`.
- **Dawn Code Counterparts (`file:line`):**
  - `bench/vendor/dawn/src/dawn/native/RingBufferAllocator.cpp` and `src/dawn/native/Queue.cpp` (`WriteBuffer`): suballocates upload staging from a persistent mapped ring buffer.

### 3.7 `owner_compute_workgroup_barrier_translation` — Single-Lane (`input4 == 0u`) Workgroup Memory Zero-Initialization, Thread-Stack Array Return-by-Value & Indirect Dispatch Guard Overhead

- **Affected Backends:** `apple-metal`, `intel-vulkan`, `windows-amd-vulkan`, `windows-d3d12`
- **Loss Count:** 27 operation losses (up to `14.19x`), 137 total phase/resource losses (up to `15.30x`) across 20 distinct workloads
- **Doe Code Owners (`file:line`):**
  - `runtime/c/compiler/targets/metal/zero.c:54-138` (`metal_zero_helpers`, `metal_workgroup_zero_helpers`) and `runtime/c/compiler/targets/metal/stage.c:319-337` (`entry_wrapper`): zero-initializes `var<workgroup>` variables serially on lane 0 (`if (input4 == 0u)`), and for non-atomic workgroup arrays calls `doe_wstore(&w0, doe_zero())`, constructing a full `array<T, N>` on thread 0's stack and copying it element-by-element into `threadgroup` memory while all other workgroup lanes stall at `threadgroup_barrier`.
  - `runtime/c/compiler/targets/hlsl/stage.c:248-261` (`entry_wrapper`) and `runtime/c/compiler/src/spirv.c:843-862` (`emit_workgroup_initialization`): zero-initializes `groupshared` / `OpVariable Workgroup` memory serially on lane 0 (`if (input4 == 0u) w0 = ...;` / `OpStore` on `lane == 0`) instead of striping zero stores across workgroup invocations.
  - `runtime/c/backends/metal/src/indirect_commands.c:76-92` (`doe_mtl_commands_dispatch_indirect`), `runtime/c/backends/vulkan/src/indirect.c`, `runtime/c/native/src/d3d12_indirect_prepare.c`: allocates a scratch buffer, copies indirect dispatch arguments, and runs a 1-thread guard compute dispatch before every `wgpuComputePassEncoderDispatchWorkgroupsIndirect`.
- **Dawn Code Counterparts (`file:line`):**
  - `bench/vendor/dawn/src/tint/lang/core/ir/transform/zero_init_workgroup_memory.cc`: stripes `var<workgroup>` zero-initialization across all `local_invocation_index` threads in parallel (`for (uint idx = local_index; idx < count; idx += wg_size)`) directly in workgroup memory without thread-local aggregate temporaries.
  - `bench/vendor/dawn/src/dawn/native/IndirectDrawValidationEncoder.cpp`: batches indirect validation across dispatches.

Each cell reports `Doe/Dawn p50 ratio [CI95_low, CI95_high]` (with `Doe p50 / Dawn p50` in ms or MiB for operation and RSS columns). Ratio `< 1.00x` means Doe C is faster/smaller than Dawn; ratio `> 1.00x` means Doe C is slower/larger than Dawn.
